def main():
    args = [a for a in sys.argv[1:] if not a.startswith("--")]
    force = "--force" in sys.argv
    target = os.path.abspath(args[0] if args else "parivaar-panchang")
    ok = 0
    for rel, (sha, b64) in FILES.items():
        if rel.startswith(("/", "\\")) or ".." in rel.replace("\\", "/").split("/"):
            sys.exit("unsafe path in bundle: " + rel)
        data = zlib.decompress(base64.b64decode("".join(b64.split())))
        if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != sha:
            sys.exit("CHECKSUM MISMATCH (file damaged in transfer): " + rel)
        dst = os.path.join(target, *rel.split("/"))
        if os.path.exists(dst) and not force:
            print("skip (exists):", rel); continue
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dst), exist_ok=True)
        with open(dst, "wb") as f:
            f.write(data)
        ok += 1
    gs = os.path.join(target, "scripts", "guard_secrets.py")
    if os.path.exists(gs):
        os.chmod(gs, 0o755)
    print(f"Unpacked {ok} of {len(FILES)} files into {target} (all checksums OK).")
    print("Next: cd into it, run git init, start Claude Code, paste Prompt 1 from MASTER_PROMPT.md.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
